A bounded official-source lookup found that Russian Highways identifies PK-5 as toll-free, publishes PK-5 chainage km 252+170â€“334+440, and a Moscow Region law assigns the A-113 section in Golitsyno km 268+001â€“273+700, including the M-1 interchange. Those ranges overlap, narrowing the likely identity of saved toll=true A-113 edges 46â€“53. The capture coordinates are nearby, but an exact coordinate/way-to-chainage join is still missing; no fare was assigned and no code/matrix changed. No route replay, route API request, or source download.
